Now, with fifteen such objects attacking simultaneously, although not comparable to the thousands of Saint phantoms in the previous life, the combined might instantly put Li Fan and his two companions' defenses in dire straits.

The Turtle Shell, Ancestral Bone, and Fate Rope—each additional one doubled the pressure.

That they did not collapse instantly already spoke volumes of their profound foundations.

Facing such a desperate situation, they harbored no thoughts of surrender.

Instead, they strained their minds, seeking a glimmer of survival amidst the impossible.

"One heart, one mind, a trinity."

Li Fan and his two companions were slowly compressed into a single point by the onslaught of the fifteen Innate Malevolence, forming a defensive triangle, their backs to each other.

One's vision became the eyes of all three. What one saw, all three observed.

One's heart, the will of all three.

The influence from different Innate Malevolence simultaneously assailed them.

However, Li Fan, at this moment, fully possessed his own creative power, combined with Shou Qiu's foresight and open-mindedness, as well as Dao De's completeness.

The boundless malevolence, like tumultuous waves, crashed upon them.

Yet, he skillfully defused it one by one, leveraging the shared will of the three.

Shou Qiu and Dao De experienced the same.

The Innate Malevolence gained a fivefold amplification, but Li Fan's defense also instantly tripled.

And as the external pressure continuously mounted, Li Fan also felt the "one heart, one mind" growing more harmonious and seamless.

He could, with the Cosmic Cycle Great Dao, weave endless reincarnations. He could also grasp Longevity, observing mountains from the sea. And he could be complete in virtue, with an inexhaustible source.

Not only did the three share "one heart, one mind" subjectively, but even the Great Daos they controlled began to acknowledge each other.

Li Fan instantly had an epiphany. In a lull between the onslaughts of the Innate Malevolence, he drew a massive amount of accumulated True Spirit from the Void Realm.

"Behold, Shou Qiu of Fan!"

His expression solemn, he pointed outside his body.

True Spirit surged, a radiant flow of light. A phantom figure slowly walked forth from it.

Its divine aura and demeanor bore a striking resemblance to Shou Qiu. Yet, its appearance was eighty percent similar to Li Fan.

It could be called Li Fan, the Shou Qiu version.

This was Li Fan, using his mastery of the Longevity Great Dao and mobilizing infinite True Spirit, to achieve Sainthood once more in an instant!

Under normal circumstances, there would never be two Saints with the same Dao path in Mountains and Seas.

But under the profound effect of "one heart, one mind," the inexplicable influence of the Return to Truth inheritance, and the immense pressure from external forces,

even the Mountains and Seas Great Dao gradually regarded Li Fan, Shou Qiu, and Dao De as one entity.

Since they were one, there was no longer any conflict to speak of.

As soon as the Shou Qiu Incarnation of Fan appeared, it joined the trio's defense.

Still one heart and one mind, without any gaps.

Its form was majestic like mountains and seas, shielding them from the external winds and rains.

"Behold, Dao De of Fan!"

Li Fan wasted no time, continuing to create his Dao De doppelganger.

Amidst the circulating two energies, the Dao De of Li Fan strode forth.

His face bore a half-smile, half-frown, both righteous and wicked.

Around his body, black and white swirled, sharing the burden of the storms endured by the group.

It wasn't just Li Fan.

At the same instant, Shou Qiu and Dao De also each manifested two doppelgangers.

Corresponding to the other two.

In a moment, six top-tier Saints were added as reinforcements.

The pressure they faced instantly lightened.

With the three main bodies at the core, the six doppelgangers enveloped them.

Wherever the defense was weakest, their combined strength converged instantly.

Where their thoughts reached, their power followed.

The result was as if Li Fan and his two companions could simultaneously wield nine times their power.

And the defensive line they formed still operated freely, without any awkwardness or flaws.

On the other hand, the Void Realm's offensive, though seemingly vast and terrifying, consisting of fifteen Innate Malevolence,

was actually fragmented, each fighting for itself, and merely fivefold duplicates.

Once they had adapted to the attack of one, the threat from the other four duplicates seemed to become less lethal.

Li Fan and his two companions, along with the six doppelgangers, began to cooperate even more seamlessly.

They could even contemplate the Dao and gain insights while simultaneously deflecting the Innate Malevolence's attacks.

"In fact, if truly pushed to the brink, and without resorting to the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood, we still have one last desperate measure."

"Just as Dao De did before achieving Sainthood, we could perform the act of splitting the Dao!"

"Each body could do so. This would instantly double our combat power!"

"However, it is easy to split but not so easy to achieve completeness again. The consequence is likely for us to fall from the Saint realm, or even have our 'one heart, one mind' shatter, leading to internal strife," Li Fan's mind churned, rapidly calculating.

Dao De agreed wholeheartedly: "But with Return to Truth, there's no need to consider so much. Let's overcome this calamity first, and observe the changes in Mountains and Seas!"

Shou Qiu gazed into the depths of the Void Realm, deep in thought: "The withdrawal of twelve Innate Malevolence has had no small impact on the Mountains and Seas battlefield. I have a premonition that the summary of Mountains and Seas changes after this Return to Truth might bring a pleasant surprise."

...

Every insight gained by the trio in battle was shared and circulated telepathically.

Compared to the Innate Malevolence, which was destined to remain unchanged from the moment it manifested in this world, Li Fan and his companions grew demonstrably stronger with each passing moment.

And their rate of improvement was astonishingly fast.

The outcome of this battle was already decided at this moment.

Provided that the mastermind behind the Void Realm did not introduce further variables.

Of course, even with "one heart, one mind," the power of the trio had a limit.

Even with Li Fan's envisioned Dao De split, they would at most add eighteen Mountains and Seas Saints.

If the Void Realm mastermind, like in the previous life, without regard for cost, summoned thousands of Saint phantoms from other temporal nodes, then Li Fan and his companions' defense would be instantly shattered.

But in this life, Li Fan and his companions had not displayed any signs related to the transformation of truth and falsehood.

In the eyes of the Void Realm mastermind, they were merely Saints who were a bit stronger and had more novel methods.

As a rational commander, it would never make such a foolish move.

Thus, after a final assessment of the current situation, it decisively chose to withdraw its forces.

Faster than they came, the Innate Malevolence and the Three Saints' Void Realm Incarnations instantly vanished without a trace.

However, a temporary retreat did not mean the Void Realm had given up its claim on the current Mountains and Seas.

Li Fan keenly noticed that as the Three Saints' Void Realm Incarnations departed, the obstructing Dao Annihilation ahead became even more profound.

Although it could not harm forces at the level of the Three Saints, it was an increasingly destructive blow to ordinary Saints and Mountains and Seas.

Since it couldn't be taken down for now, the plan was to tie it up.

Once its forces at other temporal nodes were dealt with, it would then deploy a large army and crush them with absolute strength.

On some level, the Void Realm mastermind's choice was not necessarily wrong.

It was only a pity that when the lone star appeared, even the entire Void Realm was swallowed, and then reset with the activation of Return to Truth.

Thus, it was unaware that it had relinquished the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood!

Without external threats, the atmosphere in the arena did not lighten.

The Three Saints and Li Fan's trio exchanged glances, each with their own thoughts.

There was no need for Li Fan and his companions to explain their origins.

After resolving the crisis of the Three Saints' demise, the Three Saints' divine senses they held automatically departed their bodies.

Returning to the main bodies of the Three Saints.

Thus, the Three Saints learned all the causes and consequences.

Yet, even now, they found it somewhat inconceivable that Li Fan and his two companions had successfully repelled the onslaught of fifteen times the Innate Malevolence.

"Three fellow Daoists, what great divine abilities!" Saint Gui Hai's gaze swept over them, praising.

"It was merely luck. After a prolonged battle, we were already at the end of our reserves. Had the Void Realm not timely retreated, we would have almost perished on the spot!" Li Fan said without batting an eyelid.

Shou Qiu also smiled: "We also have to thank the Three Saints for the gift of 'one heart, one mind.' Otherwise, we would never have been able to resist."

Dao De also nodded in agreement: "Had it not been for this 'one heart, one mind,' we wouldn't have known how compatible we truly are."

They knew their own strength.

As the creators of the Xuanji Ring, the Three Saints naturally understood that "one heart, one mind" could indeed multiply combat power. However, it was difficult to achieve the tenfold increase that Li Fan and his companions had displayed.

However, from their performance in the previous battle, the combat power of Li Fan's trio was already not inferior to theirs.

So it was not appropriate to press the matter.

"The Void Realm's turbulence has not yet subsided. For now, let us first reconnect Mountains and Seas."

The six exchanged glances, signifying a temporary consensus.

They turned back to face the two segments of Mountains and Seas separated by the Void Realm.

No longer the same as before.

With the Three Saints present in both segments of Mountains and Seas, and Li Fan's support,

even a hundred thousand years swallowed by Dao Annihilation could be easily restored.

Under the concerted efforts of all Saints, tendrils of Mountains and Seas returned to the Void Realm.

First, a ray of light pierced the darkness.

Then, myriad lights shone forth, illuminating everything.

Mountains and Seas revived.

At the moment of reconnection, all the Saints seated on the Other Shore experienced a surge of vitality.

Clearly, they had received immense benefits.

After all, they had gained an additional hundred thousand years of accumulation, coupled with the merit of reconnecting Mountains and Seas.

Their strength, if not doubled, had certainly advanced significantly.

Perhaps the only exception was Li Fan.

This segment of Mountains and Seas did not contain his existence, so there was no corresponding merging.

However, the commotion emanating from him was in no way inferior to the others.

Besides the disguise created by his surging accumulated True Spirit, there was also the feedback from Shou Qiu and Dao De's gains.

Now, although still seated within the Xuanji Ring, directly facing the Three Saints,

Li Fan, Shou Qiu, and Dao De had become a unified entity.

They could subtly contend with the Three Saints, and their actions no longer needed to be as timid as before.

However, it seemed that the greatest beneficiaries of this reconnection were the Three Saints, Lianshan, Guihai, and Tai Yi.

Originally, Li Fan had full confidence when facing them.

But with another reconnection of Mountains and Seas, the Three Saints once again became unfathomable.

"After all, they are ancient beings who have existed since the beginning of Mountains and Seas' evolution."

"Shou Qiu's foundation is already unimaginable, let alone the Three Saints?"

"The sum total of Mountains and Seas' civilizations, beings, and Great Daos will certainly grow stronger with the reconnection of Mountains and Seas."

"Or rather, they would recover their original strength."

Li Fan had long anticipated this.

Even so, Li Fan believed that the Three Saints would not be able to distinguish between his trio and Shou Qiu and Dao De in the short term.

With the shared goal of saving Mountains and Seas, the balance would not be easily broken.

"The reconnection of Mountains and Seas has successfully restored a hundred thousand years of time. This is truly a great fortune!"

"However, although the reconnection was successful, why do I always feel that this newly born Mountains and Seas... is a bit strange?" Bai Xiao suddenly asked, looking at the newly born hundred thousand year Mountains and Seas.

Saint Lianshan spoke: "It is not your illusion. Mountains and Seas have, after all, been separated by the Void Realm for too long. Although they can be recreated from nothingness with past脈絡 as a guide, there will inevitably be some differences between the current Mountains and Seas and the former Mountains and Seas."

"Some possibilities might no longer exist. Others might become completely unrecognizable."

"Correspondingly, the entire downstream Mountains and Seas will be slightly affected."

"However, this is beneficial, not detrimental, to the overall trend of Mountains and Seas."

Shou Qiu suddenly spoke to Li Fan in his mind: "The trace of Heavenly Net Emperor's existence has vanished."

Li Fan was astonished and looked towards the original possibilities within Mountains and Seas.

In the Primordial Immortal Realm, it should have been Heavenly Net Immortal Emperor in charge.

The newly born Mountains and Seas should have followed past inertia, recreating all past events.

But for some unknown reason, in the newly born original possibilities, the Heavenly Net Emperor was absent.

Instead, another Immortal Emperor named [Ling Xi] was in his place.

The waters of the upstream had audaciously changed course.

Causing the original possibilities to subtly shift.

Li Fan clearly saw that it was as if a blade had sliced through it, giving rise to two distinctly different possibilities.

The one that was flourishing was naturally the continuation of the newly emerged original possibility, having inherited [Ling Xi] Immortal Emperor.

While the original one Li Fan was familiar with...

Had truly become a rootless source, a water without origin. Without the infusion of power from the upstream of Mountains and Seas, it was destined to gradually wither away.

"What is the meaning of this?" Li Fan furrowed his brow slightly.

He naturally wasn't worried about his trio, who originated from the Primordial, being affected by these anomalies.

Transcendent beings were already free from the constraints of their originating possibilities, let alone those of their current strength.

"Mountains and Seas revived, but Heavenly Net is not among them."

"Could it be..."

Shou Qiu was momentarily unsure.

"What the Three Saints said earlier, that they haven't seen Heavenly Net Immortal Emperor transcend the entire Immortal Realm, might not be entirely true."

"We still need to uncover the truth for ourselves." Dao De secretly muttered.

"In the revived Mountains and Seas, there are no traces of Heavenly Net. What truly happened back then might be lost to time. We can only infer it by finding the so-called 'opportunity' that Heavenly Net Immortal Emperor once found in the lower realm from even more ancient times in Mountains and Seas." Shou Qiu's gaze turned towards the Mountains and Seas ahead.

"If my estimation is correct, it should be within the Mountains and Seas ahead."

Li Fan, hearing this, felt somewhat disoriented: "I always thought the matter of Heavenly Net Emperor was in the distant past. Yet, we have only taken two or three steps within Mountains and Seas. In the grand scheme of Mountains and Seas, it's merely an insignificant period of time."

Dao De also seemed lost in thought: "The anomaly of Heavenly Net only occurred at the end of his reign. It's likely that he changed after finding the so-called 'creation.' Indeed, we can glimpse its secret."

"However..."

"If it truly was a significant opportunity to transcend Mountains and Seas, it might be lost forever." Dao De suddenly said.

Chapter 1724: The Hidden Veins of Mountains and Seas

Li Fan nodded slightly at Shou Qiu. Even Saints who had experienced the rebirth of Mountains and Seas would not know about such profound future prospects, let alone the great opportunities that might allow the entire Immortal Realm to transcend.

"Such secrets, yet to be known. Truly a great regret," Dao De sighed.

"In Mountains and Seas, there are infinite matters. Even the Three Saints, who in a sense represent Mountains and Seas themselves, cannot know everything. We, with the transformation of truth and falsehood upon us, have already received immense fortune," Shou Qiu smiled, not bothering himself with it.

Li Fan, however, suddenly uttered something astonishing: "I have an idea. As recorded in 'Return to Truth,' the Outline of Mountains and Seas Changes might contain clues related to the True Emperor."

"It may not directly record that unknown creation opportunity, but by comparing the changes in Mountains and Seas before and after the vanishing of the Primordial Immortal Realm, we might glean something."

"I already possess the Outline of Changes for the later period. I only need to derive an Outline for the period before the Primordial Immortal Realm's disappearance. By comparing the two, we might be able to discern something."

The three shared a unified heart and mind, so they naturally knew how detailed the Outline obtained by 'Return to Truth' was. Li Fan could even use it as a template to achieve Sainthood in Mountains and Seas.

"Since 'Return to Truth' can reshape Mountains and Seas time and again, all changes within Mountains and Seas naturally escape its notice," Shou Qiu pondered for a moment and then nodded in agreement.

"If that's the case, it's simple. We merely need to reconnect again, pushing the time node back before the True Emperor's disappearance, and then use 'Return to Truth.' This way, we can both observe the impact of the Three Saints' demise on the changes in Mountains and Seas and investigate the True Emperor's secrets."

The three communicated silently.

Within the Xuanji Ring, the Saints were already discussing the next event of reconnecting Mountains and Seas. Until the threat of Mountains and Seas being annihilated was completely resolved, the Saints had no concept of rest. Fortunately, with their repeated victories, and even cooperating to escape mortal danger from Void Realm Incarnations, the morale among the Saints was high.

"The Void Realm ahead spans only thirty thousand years of time. Compared to our hundred thousand years of reconnection, it is but a trifle. However..."

"It is not something that can be easily overcome." Saint Lianshan's words immediately silenced the excited emotions stirring in the hearts of the Saints.

"Do you all know why the Void Realm manifested our incarnations to attack us in the Void Realm?"

Facing Saint Lianshan's question, all the Saints seemed to have thought of something simultaneously, their expressions subtly shifting.

"Before this reconnection of Mountains and Seas, we, who were ambushed, were not particularly special. In terms of strength, we were no stronger than beings existing at other points in time; nor did we do anything to specifically draw the attention of the Void Realm..."

"We were merely obstructed by the Void Realm on our way to the Mountains and Seas ahead."

Through these two tentative encounters with the mastermind behind the Void Realm, Li Fan had gained some understanding of its operating methods. It was an extremely calm and rational decision-maker, doing nothing without purpose. Yet, at crucial moments, it would spare no expense, gambling everything in a desperate struggle. Hearing the Three Saints speak now, Li Fan immediately understood.

The struggle between Mountains and Seas and the Void Realm was a vast battlefield. If it were an insignificant corner, the mastermind behind the Void Realm would not have diverted power without reason to intercept the Three Saints.

"That is to say..."

"The Mountains and Seas ahead are like a strategic mountain pass, a military stronghold, a crucial point that must be fought for. To prevent any possible unexpected situations, they dispatched forces to eliminate them before the Three Saints could make contact."

Li Fan and the other two exchanged glances. Shou Qiu directly asked, "May I dare to ask Saint Lianshan, what special significance do the Mountains and Seas ahead hold?"

Saint Lianshan pondered for a moment, then did not answer directly but began to explain to everyone. "Mountains and Seas are a continuous existence along the entire timeline, from antiquity to the present, and into the future. Within Mountains and Seas themselves, infinite possibilities are derived. Mountains and Seas themselves are also in constant flux."

"But the overall trend of Mountains and Seas remains unchanged. After the division of spirit in Mountains and Seas, they were interconnected, inseparable. Then, they ultimately moved towards the end of time, ushering in their final doom. This is the destiny of Mountains and Seas."

"The actions of the beings of Mountains and Seas, even ourselves as Saints, might influence numerous possibilities within Mountains and Seas. However..."

"It is difficult to affect the overall trend of Mountains and Seas!" Saint Lianshan waved his hand gently, and an image of the continuous Mountains and Seas appeared before everyone. Compared to the Mountains and Seas that the current Saints saw, the scene before them was more complete. From the inaccessible beginning of Mountains and Seas to the dark and traceless end, the continuity and length of Mountains and Seas were manifested in this manner.

"It seems that after three reconnecting events, the strength of the Three Saints has indeed greatly improved."

"Saint Lianshan's casual gesture already embodies a modicum of the essence of the 'Outline of Mountains and Seas Changes.'" Li Fan observed the scene before him, inwardly astonished. He could see the complete picture of Mountains and Seas because 'Return to Truth' bestowed upon him the Outline. But the Three Saints had gained it through their own comprehension.

Ignoring the inner thoughts of the Saints in the arena, Saint Lianshan continued his discourse. "The flow of Mountains and Seas remains unchanged in form. Even if cut apart piece by piece by the Void Realm, it still maintains its overall posture."

"Even with the immense power of the Void Realm, it is still so. What about us?"

"Mountains and Seas are difficult to change, precisely because of this." Saint Lianshan paused here. Then, he suddenly shifted his tone. "Although difficult to change, it is not impossible. We often say that certain matters in Mountains and Seas are irrelevant to the final outcome and need not be concerned about. This is not because we are careless, but because even if they exert their maximum influence, they cannot shake the overall trend of Mountains and Seas. Truly, they are 'irrelevant to the final outcome.'"

"Even if an entire segment of Mountains and Seas is annihilated, as long as the overall veins of Mountains and Seas remain, there is a possibility of rebirth."

"However, there are existences that can truly alter the form of Mountains and Seas. Throughout the long lifespan of Mountains and Seas, there have only been a few such critical junctures."

"We are currently facing one of them." Saint Lianshan's words caused all the Saints in the Xuanji Ring to become solemn. Shou Qiu recalled his past observations but did not notice anything peculiar. The other Saints in the Xuanji Ring were similarly bewildered. Lianshan seemed to perceive the inner thoughts of the Saints. "Beings possess the instinct to conceal themselves, and Mountains and Seas are no different. These few critical junctures are like the vital points of a person. Mountains and Seas will naturally conceal them."

"Even ordinary Saints, if placed within them, would find it difficult to perceive."

"Only those who can survey the entirety of Mountains and Seas, observing from a global perspective, can vaguely discern its veins. Matters concerning the lifeblood of Mountains and Seas should not be easily revealed. However, given that Mountains and Seas have already fallen into peril, this situation is now inconsequential." Saying this, Saint Lianshan made a gesture, and Saint Guihai instantly magnified a segment of the panoramic view of Mountains and Seas for all the Saints.

"As the saying goes, 'traces of snakes and ants, a thousand miles hidden.' The affairs and people who seem insignificant might have unimaginable impacts on the overall trend of Mountains and Seas. Initially unnoticeable, they may erupt after a long time." The image continued to enlarge, focusing on a specific possibility, a particular cultivator. This cultivator had already reached the realm of Transcension. He could leap out of his own possibility at any moment, yet for some unknown reason, he repeatedly looked up at the sky, sighed, and ultimately gave up. He did not truly transcend and leave. The realm of Transcension was insignificant in the eyes of the Saints. Seeing this scene, they all felt somewhat perplexed.

Li Fan, however, felt a stir in his heart. "Shou Qiu, do you find this cultivator somewhat familiar?" "Although there are only a few similarities, it should be confirmed that he is related to that person."

"The final Saint of Mountains and Seas, Qiu Xin Hui?" Dao De also discerned some clues. The Three Saints did not continue to play coy. "This cultivator is named Qiu Tian Hui. He lingered at the threshold of Transcension, not due to insufficient talent, but because..."

"Let us listen to his inner thoughts." Saint Tai Yi flicked his finger, and as a speck of light shot into the image of Mountains and Seas, merging with Qiu Tian Hui's figure. His inner thoughts then echoed in the Xuanji Ring, reaching the ears of all the Saints. "Troublesome, annoying." "To leap beyond possibility, yet still be trapped and separated within Mountains and Seas. What kind of transcendence is this?" "This step, it would have been better not to take!" "I wonder, is there a more perfect method in the world?" ... Qiu Tian Hui's inner thoughts resounded, and all the Saints raised their eyebrows, finding it somewhat unbelievable. "Before even taking the step of Transcension, he could see the truth of Mountains and Seas? From within a well, yet observe the entire heavens?" "When I transcended, I was far from this troubled. Is Truth truly Falsehood?" The Saints were shaken, and the scene of Qiu Tian Hui continued to unfold. He pondered for several years, finding no solution. So, he left his homeland and wandered in various places. Of course, he did not leave that possibility, merely changing star systems. Time flowed, and countless years passed. Qiu Tian Hui remained unkempt, appearing increasingly疯疯癫癫. This day, he suddenly sensed something, and his expression of absentmindedness instantly returned to normal. He flashed to the side of an ordinary small pond. "This place... seems somewhat strange?" All the Saints in the Xuanji Ring, some had already recognized the origin of the pond. "The Mountains and Seas Fishing Ground?" Qiu Tian Hui's initial expression was extremely solemn, but then he rejoiced, clapping his hands and exclaiming, "The path to escape lies within this!" Saying this, he leaped down and plunged into the pond water. Li Fan knew the profound mystery of the so-called Mountains and Seas Fishing Ground, also known as the Fishing Pond. Only the true angler could see the myriad mysteries within the Fishing Pond. Only by truly engaging in fishing could one hook various treasures from the Fishing Pond. And what Qiu Tian Hui did next greatly exceeded Li Fan's expectations. He did not hold a fishing rod, but used himself as bait. He lured the Mountains and Seas Angler from the other side of the Mountains and Seas Fishing Ground! "Could it be that he intends to escape Mountains and Seas with the help of the Mountains and Seas Angler?" "It's merely moving from one cage to another, perhaps even falling into the Angler's net, losing control of himself, and meeting a more tragic end." "With his perception and wisdom, he should not take such an unwise action." While the Saints were still discussing, Qiu Xin Hui had already been pulled away by the Mountains and Seas Angler in an instant. There was nothing unexpected about it. The Mountains and Seas Angler was also a Saint-level existence in Mountains and Seas. The gap between him and a cultivator who had not yet transcended was immeasurable. Qiu Tian Hui had no room to resist, which was normal. If he could contend with the Sea Angler... then he would absolutely possess a great fortune like Li Fan, who had 'Return to Truth.' Unfortunately, he did not. The Xuanji Ring fell into silence, and all the Saints looked to the Three Saints for an answer. Saint Lianshan merely signaled gently, and the Saints continued to watch. As expected, after Qiu Tian Hui disappeared and the Fishing Pond fell silent for a period, changes occurred again. The pond water churned, intensifying. It was as if something was about to emerge from the pond. After a moment, with a bang, the entire Fishing Pond exploded. This Mountains and Seas Fishing Ground vanished from this possibility, and a figure, having departed, reappeared in this place. It was Qiu Tian Hui himself! Although he seemed to have only left for a short time, Qiu Tian Hui's clothing, appearance, and even his spiritual state were vastly different from before. He bowed respectfully towards the original location of the Fishing Pond. Qiu Tian Hui looked up at the sky again, his previous confusion completely gone. "Just as I suspected before, although Mountains and Seas are separated by Dao Annihilation, as long as Mountains and Seas themselves still exist, it means that the disconnected segments of Mountains and Seas are faintly connected by veins. Ordinary means cannot perceive them. Only through various special methods can one follow these veins." "The Mountains and Seas Fishing Ground is one such method. However, it is already occupied by Senior Angler; it can no longer accommodate others. I can only find another way..." Qiu Tian Hui waved his hand, restoring the damage caused by the annihilation of the Fishing Pond to its original state, and then turned to return home. Indeed, Qiu Tian Hui had a Taoist companion. They had grown up together, supporting each other on their cultivation journey, and had reached this point together. Qiu Tian Hui's cultivation had reached the pinnacle of possibility, and his companion was only slightly inferior, having long ago attained the realm of the Nameless True Immortal. She was only half a step away from Transcension. Her name was Shao Zi Dai. Her path was one of love! Qiu Tian Hui had disappeared for several years, his whereabouts unknown, his life or death uncertain. However, Shao Zi Dai had never worried that he would leave her someday. She firmly believed that no matter what happened, Qiu Tian Hui would eventually return. At this moment, Qiu Tian Hui reappeared, their eyes met, and a thousand unspoken words were conveyed in a gentle embrace. This was followed by a passionate spiritual and physical union. Qiu Tian Hui's experiences were all known to Shao Zi Dai during this cultivation. "In that case, Senior Angler is truly a pure angler. Only interested in the act of fishing itself, not the prey. It's quite fortunate that you could escape..." Shao Zi Dai murmured with a light laugh, nestled in Qiu Tian Hui's embrace. Qiu Tian Hui said with some self-satisfaction, "Where is it mere luck? The moment I saw that Mountains and Seas Fishing Ground, I discerned the true nature of the Angler on the other side. That's why I dared to use myself as bait and venture into the unknown." "Then has Husband thought of a complete plan?" Shao Zi Dai did not refute but asked again. Qiu Tian Hui's expression suddenly turned solemn. "I already have a vague idea. That is..." "Mountains and Seas Together!" "Mountains and Seas Together? What does these four words mean?" Not only Shao Zi Dai, but all the Saints in the Xuanji Ring could not help but lend their ears. "The veins of Mountains and Seas run through antiquity and into the distant, unknowable future." "For us to transcend, to take this step, we should not cross into the interior of Mountains and Seas. Instead, we should walk along the direction of Mountains and Seas' life," Qiu Tian Hui said, his eyes bright, pointing upwards.

Chapter 1725: Giving Birth to a Saint Alive

Qiu Tianhui’s gesture, perhaps by mere coincidence, happened to be directed towards the Saints observing from outside the projected image.

The Saints remained impassive, listening as Qiu Tianhui continued.

“Mountains and Seas have existed continuously since antiquity, like a rushing river. To be immersed within it, it is naturally difficult to alter its form; one can only follow the flow.”

“Ordinary methods of transcendence involve leaping out and standing above the river. If one is sufficiently strong and positioned high enough, one can then survey the entire panorama of Mountains and Seas!”

“However, situated in the later stages of Mountains and Seas’ life cycle, many opportunities have been depleted, and hidden powerful beings lie in wait. Even those of extraordinary talent would find it difficult to change this situation.”

Shao Zidai cast her gaze beyond the heavens, as if perceiving the true state of Mountains and Seas, and nodded slightly in agreement. “Then, what is the method of transcendence your husband speaks of?”

“To align with the form of Mountains and Seas, and harmonize with its intent. Continuing like the ever-present, spontaneously regenerating. Then one’s fate will be intertwined with Mountains and Seas, and one’s spirit will coexist with Mountains and Seas,” Qiu Tianhui’s answer was exceedingly esoteric.

Yet, Shao Zidai seemed to be in perfect sync with his thoughts. As her eyes shifted, she understood his intention.

With a gentle wave of her hand, a phantom image of a rushing river appeared before them.

The image focused on the underwater, where a nascent undercurrent grew vigorously. Following the direction of the river’s flow, it strived to extend and perpetuate itself.

“My husband’s meaning is to be like this undercurrent, utilizing the continuous momentum of Mountains and Seas to bolster our lifespans.”

Qiu Tianhui smiled slightly. “You understand me best, Zidai!”

His expression was filled with pride. “The lifespans of beings within Mountains and Seas are finite. Even so-called transcendent experts cannot escape the tribulation of decay. Yet, this method borrows the grand momentum of Mountains and Seas to extend one’s own lifespan. For as long as Mountains and Seas endure, one’s life will continue indefinitely, free from the anxieties of ordinary lifespans!”

“And as one’s own life extends, one occupies more of Mountains and Seas. Until…”

Qiu Tianhui pointed at the vision Shao Zidai had conjured. Originally, the undercurrent was only as thick as a thumb.

But as it continually surged forward, gathering more and more surrounding vapor, its form gradually thickened.

By the end of the river, it had actually come to occupy half of the underwater territory.

It was almost on par with the entirety of Mountains and Seas!

“This is what is meant by ‘Mountains and Seas Together’!”

Shao Zidai gazed at the scene her husband had conjured for her, captivated and lost in thought.

“It sounds plausible, but is it truly feasible?” After a long silence, she still harbored some doubts.

“I’ve heard that Mountains and Seas are separated by Dao Annihilation. Are we not like fish trapped in a pond? How can we achieve continuous existence like Mountains and Seas themselves?”

“Ordinary methods of transcendence are fraught with immense difficulty. Let alone this unconventional method of transcendence. It is akin to stealing the divine essence of Mountains and Seas and altering its very form. Without great fortune, great perseverance, and great destiny, it cannot be accomplished.”

“Given our origins as beings of Mountains and Seas, attempting such a rebellious act is like throwing an egg against a rock. However…”

“My dear, do not forget that Mountains and Seas are not eternally prosperous.Judging from the current scene of Dao Annihilation raging, it seems Mountains and Seas are gradually losing the battle. Mountains and Seas are in decline and are struggling to manage themselves. This presents us with an opportunity!”

Shao Zidai vaguely understood Qiu Tianhui’s intention.

But then she frowned slightly. “Together with Mountains and Seas. Yet Mountains and Seas are ultimately in decline. This method…”

Qiu Tianhui chuckled. “Where in the world is there a perfect method? My dear, you should not be too greedy. Given our origins, if we are fortunate enough to cultivate to a level of parity with Mountains and Seas, that would already be more than enough. Furthermore…”

“Let’s take it one step at a time. Once we reach that level, perhaps new methods will emerge. A living being will not be suffocated to death.”

Shao Zidai also laughed, no longer dwelling on the issue.

The two then spent several hundred years intimately discussing this so-called “Mountains and Seas Together” method, never leaving their cave dwelling.

Then, the scene abruptly cut off.

Within the Xuanji Ring, all the Saints looked at the Three Saints in bewilderment.

They did not understand the meaning.

“This method of ‘Mountains and Seas Together,’ while sounding plausible, requires immense effort to truly succeed… Even with our talents, it seems difficult,” Prying Opportunity frowned deeply, shaking his head after careful consideration. “But since the Three Saints said it is a crucial node of the Mountains and Seas’ veins… could it be that he actually succeeded?” Bai Xiao was somewhat incredulous.

“But if he really succeeded, why have we never seen these two individuals in the subsequent Mountains and Seas?” Judgment asked again, scanning the current Mountains and Seas outside the Xuanji Ring.

Indeed, Qiu Tianhui and Shao Zidai were nowhere to be found among them.

“Are they truly absent?”

“Although Qiu Tianhui is not present, there is Li Tianhui and Zhang Tianhui. Although Shao Zidai is not present, there is Xia Zidai and Bai Zidai.”

As Saint Lianshan spoke, numerous light spots appeared scattered throughout the Mountains and Seas.

Following their gaze, they saw beings across different possibilities within Mountains and Seas.

Their origins, circumstances, realms, and strengths were all different.

If Saint Lianshan had not collectively marked them, even with the combined eyesight of the Saints present, it would have been difficult to discern their commonalities.

“Could it be that these individuals are incarnations of Qiu Tianhui and Shao Zidai? Impossible!” The Saints, confident in their own perception, observed carefully for another moment and then unanimously shook their heads in disbelief.

At this moment, Shou Qiu spoke, “They are not Qiu Tianhui and Shao Zidai themselves, but their descendants. Indeed, a remarkable technique!”

“Descendants?” The Saints were stunned and looked again.

However, they became even more confused.

“Shou Qiu Gong, what do you mean by that? If there is a blood relationship between them, we would not be unable to distinguish it.”

Shou Qiu Gong said, “Of course, I am not referring to ordinary blood descendants. Rather…”

“If Qiu Tianhui and Shao Zidai did not exist, these beings would not have appeared in Mountains and Seas. By borrowing the power of Mountains and Seas, they gave birth to these beings. This can also be called their parents.”

“Precisely so.” Saint Guihai spoke up in agreement at this moment, providing the answer.

“Giving birth to beings by borrowing the power of Mountains and Seas?” The Saints savored these words and slowly began to understand what Qiu Tianhui and Shao Zidai had been doing in their cave dwelling for hundreds of years.

They were not indulging in physical pleasure but were truly cultivating and comprehending the Dao!

“The evolution of beings in Mountains and Seas is one of the fundamental Daos of Mountains and Seas itself. How could Qiu Tianhui and his wife meddle in it?” The Saints were all somewhat shocked, looking towards Saint Guihai.

Saint Guihai’s expression was impassive. “It is not exactly meddling. Just as Qiu Tianhui said before, they are merely borrowing the momentum of Mountains and Seas. As long as Mountains and Seas exist for a day, they will continue to evolve beings. This is one of the hidden important veins of Mountains and Seas.”

“This couple is indeed exceptionally talented, to have actually glimpsed its mystery and intertwined their power within it.”

Saint Lianshan pointed to various spots on the panoramic view of Mountains and Seas again. Countless light spots, like stars, lit up simultaneously. They were dense and covered the entire Mountains and Seas! From the Mountains and Seas where Qiu Tianhui originated, all the way to the end of time. Although Mountains and Seas were gradually declining, the starlight never ceased!

“As expected!”

Li Fan squinted his eyes and looked at the end of Mountains and Seas, recalling Qiu Xinhui, the last Saint of Mountains and Seas.

“At the end of time, the Great Dao of Mountains and Seas had withered to such an extent, yet such an astonishing figure as the Last Saint, Qiu Xinhui, was born, as if he alone occupied most of the spiritual energy at the end of Mountains and Seas. Yet, his origin was ordinary, with no family heritage. If Qiu Tianhui’s ‘Mountains and Seas Together’ method is the crystallization condensed from the end of Mountains and Seas after a lifetime, then it makes sense.”

Li Fan, Shou Qiu, and Dao De shared the same heart and mind. As their thoughts turned rapidly, Li Fan’s idea was met with agreement from the other two.

“This method of Qiu Tianhui’s, borrowing the momentum of Mountains and Seas to strengthen oneself. The weaker Mountains and Seas become, the stronger he becomes. Until the end of time, when Mountains and Seas are at their weakest, his power can be maximized. The Last Saint, Qiu Xinhui, truly fulfilled what Qiu Tianhui demonstrated countless years ago, acting as an equal counterweight to Mountains and Seas itself. He could even do things that Mountains and Seas could not, single-handedly changing the tide and saving Mountains and Seas from Dao Annihilation.” Shou Qiu commented.

“However, Qiu Tianhui and Qiu Xinhui do not seem to be the same person. This so-called method of transcendence…”

“It’s truly hard to say,” Dao De mused.

Meanwhile, in the Xuanji Ring, Saint Lianshan spoke again. “Qiu Tianhui and his wife, although they hid in the forefront Mountains and Seas, have directly influenced the crucial vein of the evolution of beings in Mountains and Seas, connecting them across millennia.”

“Mountains and Seas are constantly changing. The evolution and birth of beings are also ceaseless. Billions of beings, compared to Mountains and Seas, are merely ants. But after all, they are born from Mountains and Seas and grow within Mountains and Seas. If such a large number of beings were to instantly disappear on the scale of Mountains and Seas…”

“It would be enough to cause severe damage to Mountains and Seas. More importantly…”

Saint Lianshan’s expression became solemn. “We still do not know how Qiu Tianhui and his wife intervened in the vein of the evolution of beings in Mountains and Seas. If this method is controlled by the Void Realm…”

“Then the Void Realm could, just like Qiu Tianhui, instantly deploy countless clones of Dao Annihilation throughout the interior of Mountains and Seas. The defenses of Mountains and Seas would likely be riddled with holes in an instant.”

“This is also why the Mountains and Seas ahead are such an important node.”

After the explanation from the Three Saints, everyone finally understood.

“May I ask Saint Lianshan, what is the actual strength of this Qiu Tianhui? Logically speaking, being able to interfere with the Great Dao of Beings in Mountains and Seas would at least require Saint cultivation. Yet, his method of transcendence seems unable to feed back to himself…” Prying Opportunity said uncertainly.

It was Saint Guihai who answered.

Guihai pointed to the myriad beings like stars in the panoramic view of Mountains and Seas. “These beings were born because of them, and they are the crystallization of this couple’s Dao. The more beings there are, the stronger their power naturally becomes. They do not need the so-called ordinary methods of feedback, because they are borrowing the momentum of Mountains and Seas, so each birth of a being represents an improvement in their cultivation.”

“Therefore, in the previous scene, we only observed so much. At a certain moment, they suddenly disappeared from our sight, which means that through continuous accumulation, they are no longer inferior to us.”

The Saints were instantly silenced upon hearing this.

With their knowledge and experience, they found it truly difficult to imagine that there was such an unconventional cultivation method in Mountains and Seas.

Directly leaping from the realm of Nameless True Immortal to a Saint of Mountains and Seas. And not just any Saint, but a top-tier one.

“What a grand method of borrowing the momentum of Mountains and Seas. I see that Mountains and Seas are declining day by day, and I suppose these two are partly responsible,” Saint Xuanpin snorted coldly, filled with indignation.

Saint Tai Yi smiled. “The demise of Mountains and Seas began with the story of devouring the Gods in the past. Due to the act of splitting their spirits back then, Dao Annihilation emerged. The merging, combining, and eventual dissolution of Mountains and Seas is their destined fate. It has nothing to do with Qiu Tianhui and his wife.”

“Even without Qiu Tianhui, the fate of Mountains and Seas at the end of time would not have been much better.”

“Strictly speaking, this couple, with mortal bodies, differentiated, stole, and occupied parts of the Great Dao of Mountains and Seas. As their Dao was achieved, they can be considered a part of Mountains and Seas.”

Xuanpin’s indignant expression instantly vanished. Tai Yi’s words had already made it very clear. In a sense, Qiu Tianhui and his wife were of the same nature as Lianshan, Guihai, and Tai Yi. It was just that one was a manifestation of the Great Dao of Mountains and Seas, while the other had stolen and seized partial control of the Great Dao of Mountains and Seas through beings.

“Since they are a part of Mountains and Seas, they should be able to jointly resist the Void Realm.”

“However, the reality is not so.”

“Do you all remember Qiu Tianhui’s words earlier, ‘take it one step at a time’?”

“I don’t know what he saw, but facing the conflict between Mountains and Seas and the Void Realm, he directly chose to hide.” Saint Lianshan said with a smile.

“We can only be sure that the couple is still in the Mountains and Seas ahead. But we don’t know where they are hiding.”

Upon hearing this, the Saints couldn’t help but frown. With strength comparable to the Three Saints, if they truly intended to hide, it would indeed be difficult to find them.

“Could it be that even if Mountains and Seas fall, they can still hide indefinitely?” Li Fan suddenly blurted out.

“Daoist Universe Cycle, don’t speak in anger. Although they are difficult to find, there are still traces to follow. If Mountains and Seas were truly swallowed by Dao Annihilation, the Void Realm would definitely dispatch forces from various timelines to seize the secrets of Mountains and Seas’ beings. The situation would become even more difficult to handle,” Bai Xiao said with a bitter smile.

The Saints pondered amongst themselves, and finally, their gazes converged back on the Three Saints.

“What is the plan now?”

“Not long ago, the Void Realm attempted to kill us and failed. However, their containment force on the Mountains and Seas ahead has not diminished but increased. Although we have the boost of True Spirit Infusion, reconnecting Mountains and Seas will not be as easy as before.”

“In addition to Dao Annihilation’s invasion, any Void Realm Incarnations could launch surprise attacks.”

Upon hearing this, all the Saints present, except for Li Fan and his companions, changed their expressions slightly.

Saint Lianshan continued, “Forcibly reconnecting Mountains and Seas might provoke extreme reactions from the Void Realm. We need to proceed slowly.”

“On one hand, we need to ensure the safety of the Mountains and Seas ahead.”

“On the other hand…”

“We need to find Qiu Tianhui and his wife.”

“They are the biggest variables in this battle. If they are not under control…”

Saint Lianshan’s voice became a little deeper.

“Although they are hiding, they have never stopped borrowing the momentum of beings. We can start from here to track their whereabouts,” Saint Guihai added.

“Ordinary methods cannot trace the beings of Mountains and Seas. Only by constantly reincarnating within Mountains and Seas. If we happen to encounter Qiu Tianhui and his wife during the ‘birth of beings’…”

“There will be a connection. We can then trace their whereabouts backwards.”

The Three Saints exchanged glances and then looked at Li Fan, saying, “The Void Realm Incarnation is specifically targeting us. Naturally, we shall deal with it ourselves.”

“And with Daoist Fan presiding over Mountains and Seas, it should remain safe.” The Three Saints’ answer was within Li Fan’s expectations, but he shook his head slightly.

“I can enlighten Saints, but I cannot protect Mountains and Seas.”

“Firstly, after continuous enlightenments, even Mountains and Seas have become somewhat depleted. Secondly, as you all saw earlier, the Map of Mountains and Seas only possesses the ‘form’ of Mountains and Seas, not its substance. To withstand the residual waves of battle…”

“I fear my strength is insufficient.” Li Fan said truthfully.

Chapter 1726: The End Reaches the Beginning

"A move that annihilated three Saints, astonishing everyone present!"

The Three Saints were filled with uncontrollable shock. Even though they knew Shou Qiu possessed the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood, they had never expected his transformation to be so powerful!

As Shou Qiu erased the Void Realm Incarnations in the surrounding void, the Three Saints noticed his physical form becoming transparent. They deduced it was the price of forcefully activating his Great Dao. However, Shou Qiu's comprehension of the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood had not yet reached its peak.

Shou Qiu continued to dominate the battlefield, erasing Void Realm Incarnations with ease. He mused that with "Return to Truth," this process would have been much simpler. He felt a greater storm brewing from the Void Realm. He recognized Dao Annihilation as pawns and Void Realm Incarnations as generals, but the next arrivals would be high-ranking generals.

The Void Realm was concentrating its forces to attack this segment of Mountains and Seas, forming a monstrous, pressure-filled tsunami. Shou Qiu stood firm, shouldering the terrifying pressure, his body becoming increasingly dim and transparent. He reflected on the profoundness of the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood and the self-destructive price of its use. Li Fan, hidden in the shadows, felt Shou Qiu's words resonate deeply and resolved to act according to his heart now that he had the strength to contend with the Three Saints.

The pressure from the Void Realm suddenly lightened, replaced by a figure confronting Shou Qiu—an exact manifestation of Shou Qiu himself, condensed from the data of countless destroyed Shou Qius. Shou Qiu realized this opponent was a part of himself, formed by the collective efforts of countless Void Realm Incarnations, designed to stall or assimilate him.

The Void Realm Shou Qiu attacked with world-shattering power, trapping Shou Qiu in a vortex of destruction. Shou Qiu activated the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood to protect himself, but his attempt to negate the Void Realm Shou Qiu was less effective, as this opponent had gained thickness from the accumulation of countless paper figures. Shou Qiu devised a countermeasure: a mutually destructive move to cut down the high-ranking general and perhaps force the mastermind to reveal themselves.

He abandoned his defenses, allowing himself to be assimilated while unleashing a "Blade of Truth is Falsehood" upon himself. This self-slaughter, smoother than carving an ox, caused Shou Qiu's projection to dim. The Void Realm Shou Qiu was shocked, but the ripple effect of "Truth is Falsehood" traversed time, slaying the past, present, and future Shou Qius, fueling the Void Realm Shou Qiu's self-immolation. Shou Qiu laughed heartily as he perished, turning everything into nothingness.

Shou Qiu's demise was a chain reaction, nullifying the Void Realm's strike and causing the transformation of truth and falsehood to spread uncontrollably, disintegrating the Void Realm. The Three Saints realized the Void Realm could be erased by "Truth and Falsehood." However, they felt a greater sense of crisis as faint black threads, like spider silk, wove a net, halting the Void Realm's disintegration and patching it up. A single thread emerged, originating from Shou Qiu's demise and heading towards Li Fan's hiding place. The Void Realm, realizing Shou Qiu was merely a probing piece, was attempting to find the true Lord of the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood.

Chapter 1727: The Invisible Net Descends from the Sky

The Void Net enveloped the battlefield. Li Fan, who had silently recited 'Return to Truth' upon Shou Qiu's demise, found his hiding place locked by the Void Net. The rhythmic poundings of an invisible hammer interrupted his casting process, causing cracks in the white mist of 'Return to Truth.' The Void Realm mastermind had found an opportunity.

Li Fan remained calm, drawing upon Shou Qiu's detached state of mind. He understood that the rhythmic poundings only interrupted his casting and that the Void Realm mastermind couldn't stop 'Return to Truth' but could prolong the brief moment. He recognized the black threads invading were akin to Yuan Shi Bai Gu's methods in a past life. The Void Realm mastermind knew they couldn't stop 'Return to Truth' but wanted to replace Li Fan.

Li Fan coldly snorted and began to eliminate the disruptive influence. He used his fingertips like a brush, writing in the air with strokes mirroring the invading black lines. "When truth becomes falsehood, falsehood also becomes truth!" Each stroke erased a method of the Void Realm mastermind. Under 'Concentric Unity,' Shou Qiu's enlightenment became Li Fan's enlightenment. He could now do as Shou Qiu did, annihilating Saints' incarnations and repelling Void Realm attacks. Unlike Shou Qiu, Li Fan, having undergone hundreds of 'Return to Truth' baptisms, ignored the negative effects of 'Truth is Falsehood.'

His writing became smoother, erasing all black lines. He declared the authority of the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood, transcending all. The white mist then swallowed all light, and darkness returned. Li Fan, however, forced his eyes open, traversing towards the end of Mountains and Seas, waiting for the appearance of the 'Stars.' He realized only the Stars were worth 'Return to Truth's' action.

Fatigue washed over him, a weariness born from being consumed. Li Fan realized he was being consumed, his True Spirit depleting, experiencing a feeling of death. The transcendent state brought by Shou Qiu had receded. Fear, confusion, and doubt overshadowed his drowsiness, maintaining only a sliver of consciousness.

After an unknown period, the consumption stopped. He sensed the end of Mountains and Seas, a ray of starlight piercing the darkness. Mountains and Seas, and the Void Realm, reappeared. Li Fan fell into a deep sleep.

Anchor Point Year 1. Li Fan, upon regaining consciousness, was initially confused but then recalled Shou Qiu's sacrifice, the Void Realm mastermind, and the True Spirit extraction. The hollow feeling remained. He called out to 'Return to Truth,' demanding an explanation. He realized previous activations were similar, but this time he witnessed the entire process. He pondered why 'Return to Truth' was attached to him, its purpose unknown.

He accepted this reality, realizing 'Return to Truth' would activate even after his death. He had glimpsed the truth of its activation, but it brought more doubts. Why extract his True Spirit to recreate Mountains and Seas? Where did his infinite True Spirit come from? The hollow feeling had disappeared, but the near-death experience was vivid. He realized his foundation should be built excluding 'Return to Truth,' not including it.

He sat on Jieli Mountain for years, accepting his situation. He practiced "Truth is Falsehood, Falsehood is Truth," realizing his comprehension of 'Truth is Falsehood' far surpassed 'Falsehood is Truth.' He could erase Void Realm's effects but not generate disasters out of thin air. He concluded the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood was pieced together by two Daos: 'Truth is Falsehood, Falsehood is Truth' and 'Return to Truth.' He felt he needed to reach a higher state, like God or the Stars, to unravel mysteries. The desire to break free from all shackles reignited. He decided to inherit Shou Qiu Gong's memories and await changes within the Xuanji Ring.

Chapter 1728: Great Micro Holy Emperor's Transformation

Li Fan, Shou Qiu, and Dao De entered the Void Realm. The Three Saints and other Saints observed, discussing the variable Shou Qiu might bring. Shou Qiu, having merged with his other self, headed towards the battle. Li Fan, Shou Qiu, and Dao De hid in the shadows, observing the six-Saint battle.

When the Void Realm Incarnations unleashed their primordial malevolence—the Fate Rope binding Lianshan, the Turtle Shell suppressing Guihai, and the Ancestral Bone capturing Tai Yi—the Three Saints were forced to unleash their own. A stalemate ensued.

Li Fan, Shou Qiu, and Dao De, witnessing the Three Saints' hesitation, realized they feared severing connections within Mountains and Seas, not death itself. Shou Qiu speculated that their primordial malevolence, originating from a fallen True God, might pierce the Void Realm's obstruction and expand to the entire Mountains and Seas.

The Void Realm Incarnations, with near-limitless power, attacked recklessly. The Three Saints, though disadvantaged, held their ground. Li Fan and his companions, knowing the Three Saints' fated demise, patiently observed, gaining insights into the Daos of Mountains and Seas. Li Fan felt his next life as a Saint of Mountains and Seas would be even more powerful.

As the battle culminated, the Three Saints' 'Lines of Mountains and Seas' began to erode. New primordial malevolence from the Void Realm descended upon their main bodies. Shou Qiu, with Guihai's divine essence, moved to rescue Tai Yi. Li Fan, embodying Lianshan's divine essence, aided Guihai, while Dao De, with Tai Yi's divine essence, helped Lianshan.

The Three Saints, sensing their own spiritual senses on Li Fan and his companions, understood their method: "Using divine essence to restrain, using spiritual sense to subdue." The primordial malevolence, embodying the resentment of discarded remnants of Mountains and Seas, attacked fiercely. Li Fan described the situation as "mountains collapsing and seas boiling," a world-ending catastrophe. He used the Universe Cycle Great Dao to resolve the pressure, pushing his comprehension to its limit.

Shou Qiu easily blocked the Ancestral Bone, while Dao De, though weaker, continuously dissolved the Fate Rope's influence with Li Fan's True Spirit replenishment. The Void Realm mastermind, seeing the primordial malevolence neutralized, fell into contemplation. After a moment, twelve more instances of primordial malevolence attacked. Li Fan, realizing using the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood would lead to the same outcome as before, decided to resist head-on. He declared they would test their limits, drawing inspiration from his hundred lifetimes of foundation building. Shou Qiu and Dao De agreed, their confidence bolstered. The Void Realm mastermind, seeing Li Fan and his companions' strength and their concealed use of the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood, decided to withdraw its forces, deeming it not worth the cost.

Chapter 1729: The Mountains and Seas Are Extreme and Devour

The Void Realm's forces withdrew, but the Dao Annihilation barrier ahead intensified. Li Fan sensed the Void Realm's plan to delay until other temporal nodes were resolved. He reflected on the irony of the Void Realm abandoning the very place where the Great Dao of Truth and Falsehood resided.

The Three Saints, their imminent doom averted, acknowledged Li Fan's group's strength. Li Fan, downplaying his own abilities, credited the 'shared heart and mind' with Shou Qiu and Dao De. The Three Saints, impressed by their amplified combat power, decided to proceed with reconnecting Mountains and Seas.

With Li Fan's support in True Spirit Infusion and the combined efforts of six Saints, the hundred thousand years of separated Mountains and Seas were reconnected. All Saints benefited, their strength significantly increasing. Li Fan, however, noticed a change: the True Emperor's existence had vanished from the newly formed Mountains and Seas, replaced by an Immortal Emperor named [Lingxi]. This divergence into two possibilities worried Li Fan and Shou Qiu. They speculated that the True Emperor's fate at the end of his reign, possibly linked to an opportunity for transcendence, might hold the key. Shou Qiu suggested that the "Manual of Mountains and Seas Changes," combined with the 'Return to Truth,' might offer clues.

Chapter 1730: The Spiderweb Splits and Changes

Li Fan revealed his plan: to use 'Return to Truth' to derive a version of the 'Manual of Mountains and Seas Changes' from before the Primordial Immortal Realm's disappearance and compare it with the current version to deduce secrets concerning the True Emperor. The Saints, focused on the upcoming reconnection and the Void Realm threat, discussed the strategic importance of the thirty thousand years of Void Realm ahead. Saint Lianshan explained that certain segments of Mountains and Seas were critical junctures, vital points that the Void Realm sought to control.

The Void Realm had intercepted the Three Saints because the mountains and seas ahead were strategically vital. Lianshan explained that Mountains and Seas, though constantly changing, had an overall, immutable trend of decline towards doom. However, certain critical junctures, like the one ahead, could alter this trend. These junctures were hidden, even from ordinary Saints.

Guihai magnified a segment of the Mountains and Seas view, focusing on a cultivator named Qiu Tianhui, who hesitated before transcendence, contemplating a more perfect method. His inner thoughts revealed his dissatisfaction with the limitations of transcendence and his search for a new path. He ultimately used himself as bait in the "Mountains and Seas Fishing Ground" to attract the Mountains and Seas Angler, hoping to escape or find a new way.

The Angler, a Saint-level existence, easily captured Qiu Tianhui. However, the Fishing Pond exploded, and Qiu Tianhui reappeared, having found another way—"Mountains and Seas Together." He explained this method involved using the continuous momentum of Mountains and Seas to extend one's life, eventually occupying a significant portion of Mountains and Seas. He believed the current decline of Mountains and Seas offered an opportunity.

The Saints were perplexed. Shou Qiu revealed that Qiu Tianhui and Shao Zidai were not directly present but had influenced the evolution of beings in Mountains and Seas, creating descendants like Li Tianhui and Zhang Tianhui, who embodied their Dao. These descendants, not ordinary blood relatives, were born through borrowing Mountains and Seas' power. Saint Guihai confirmed this, explaining Qiu Tianhui and Shao Zidai had intertwined their power with the Dao of Beings. Saint Lianshan noted their descendants covered the entire Mountains and Seas, demonstrating the continuity of their method.

Li Fan connected this to Qiu Xinhui, the Last Saint, who emerged from a withered Great Dao of Mountains and Seas. He theorized Qiu Tianhui's method, culminating at the end of Mountains and Seas, created a powerful being like Qiu Xinhui. The Three Saints acknowledged the couple's talent but noted their fear of the Void Realm and their subsequent hiding. They planned to track them through their influence on beings, using reincarnation as a potential connection point. Li Fan, however, stated his inability to protect Mountains and Seas, fearing his strength was insufficient against subsequent Void Realm attacks.

Chapter 1731: Devour Mountains and Seas

Like plucking at lute strings, Li Fan attempted to touch and alter the rules of this world with his twin fingers.

"Using the power of Devouring Mountains and Seas, weave a giant net. Evolve it into a world, forcing the creatures that enter to fight desperately with all their might..."

"When a creature perishes within the net, it will instinctively absorb them."

Li Fan looked again at the combatants locked in mortal combat.

As if after their demise, one glowing orb after another floated out. Each represented a life on the battlefield, containing the principles and creations they mastered in life.

Perhaps not all, but certainly the most essential essences, brought forth by desperate life-or-death struggles that unleashed their full potential.

On the mountain of corpses and sea of blood created by the great battle, countless glowing orbs of varying sizes rose, drifting into the [Net] hanging in the sky. This scene was indescribably bizarre.

Li Fan seemed mesmerized, flicking his fingers several times, trying to alter the direction of the glowing orbs.

He failed repeatedly but never gave up.

By the time the great battle ended, although Li Fan had not succeeded even once, a sense of confidence gradually bloomed in his eyes.

As per usual, he did not collect anything for himself.

He only took back what was divided amongst the enlightened beings after their desperate battles.

The remaining enlightened beings did not express excessive gratitude towards Li Fan. They merely seized every opportunity to recover and strengthen themselves.

As indigenous inhabitants of this world, they clearly understood that the battles to come would inevitably be more brutal than the last, until they were completely annihilated.

However, the instinct for survival did not allow them to give up.

Even with only a sliver of hope, they would continue to fight!

"These are the attributes bestowed upon them by the Net of [Tether]. It has nothing to do with my incitement. This conveniently suits me."

The enlightened beings had not fully recovered when the next wave of enemies descended.

"According to the formation, resist the enemy!"

Li Fan's spiritual sense rippled outwards.

In the minds of the enlightened beings, a profound formation simultaneously materialized.

Although its principles were difficult to comprehend, it did not prevent them from entering the formation in order.

With Li Fan as the core center of the formation, all the enlightened beings were connected as one.

Killing intent condensed, forming blood-red clouds that shot up to the sky, obscuring the heavens.

This formation was created by Li Fan, based on the 'Concentric Unity' characteristic of the Xuanji Ring.

Under the enhancement of the great formation, the enlightened beings shared their senses, as if they were a single entity.

Although there was no increase in their strength or realm, it transformed countless desperate fighters into an integrated whole.

They no longer had to worry about attacks from behind, and their companions' support always arrived in the most timely manner.

From fighting individually, they automatically differentiated into countless small groups to efficiently encircle and annihilate the enemy.

The battlefield situation instantly shifted.

What was originally a balanced, intense battle, turned into a one-sided slaughter due to the emergence of this formation.

Li Fan had anticipated this outcome and his attention remained focused on the sky of this world.

"Many thanks to our elder for bestowing this method!"

Only when the battle was won and the enlightened beings shouted in unison did he slightly shift his gaze.

"There are defeated remnants, you may devour them."

Li Fan pointed at the blood-soaked battlefield and said indifferently.

The enlightened beings were all slightly stunned.

But they were created to be disposable combat constructs, so they didn't overthink it.

Upon hearing Li Fan's command, they immediately began to feast.

Soon, the messy battlefield was cleaned up.

The battlefield remnants, apart from slightly filling their stomachs, seemed to have no other significant use.

For the vast majority of the enlightened beings, this was the case.

Yet, there were a few exceptions.

"You, you, you."

"Come here."

Li Fan singled them out, and three or five figures quickly flew before him.

They knelt respectfully.

Although Li Fan's cultivation level still appeared mortal on the surface, the subtle aura emanating from him instinctively made them tremble with submission.

They dared not act presumptuously in the slightest.

"So that's how it is."

After observing for a long time, it was unclear what Li Fan saw, but he suddenly uttered this sentence.

A moment later, one of the kneeling figures, without any warning, suddenly exploded, shattering into pieces of flesh and blood.

"You all consume this."

Li Fan commanded the remaining figures.

Their bodies trembled slightly. Without hesitation, they began to swallow the remains greedily.

However, this time, as they fed, Li Fan's fingers repeatedly drew slow arcs in the void.

As if touching, and as if playing an instrument.

As the flesh and blood entered their bodies, these kneeling figures were all strengthened to varying degrees.

And at this moment, the next wave of enemies arrived.

"Kill."

Without further words, Li Fan simply gave the order, and the created beings clashed with the beasts of the net once more.

This time, Li Fan did not gain an advantage. The strength of the newly evolved creatures, taking into account the enhancement of the 'Concentric Unity' formation, was more than a notch stronger than the previous wave of enemies.

However, this did not include the slight reinforcement the enlightened beings gained from devouring the flesh and blood earlier.

This resulted in Li Fan's side still holding a slight advantage in the situation.

And...

As the battle raged, Li Fan's consciousness continued to echo.

The enlightened beings were astonished to discover that as the enemies fell, wisps of indescribable and unfathomable power were captured and absorbed by them.

Some merely had a simple effect of increasing their blood energy and cultivation, while others contained fleeting memories of insights into divine abilities and great daos!

Although initially ignorant and lacking consciousness, the enlightened beings, as Li Fan's enlightenment was completed, their intellect grew day by day. They were also naturally adept at divine abilities and combat.

Without Li Fan needing to actively teach them, many among them instantly comprehended the memories.

Even if a few remained ignorant, through shared insights, they quickly grasped the profound mysteries from their companions.

The battle transitioned from a mere contest of strength and blood energy to one where divine abilities began to manifest.

The battle was swiftly concluded.

If the initial situation was only a slight advantage, then after the enlightened beings began to comprehend and manifest divine abilities, it became a crushing victory again.

The enlightened beings, having gained benefits, became like hungry wolves, scrambling for the remaining scraps on the battlefield.

But there was too little meat and too many wolves, not enough to go around.

Thus, unprecedentedly, they began to anticipate the next wave of approaching enemies even more!

A peculiar emotion and atmosphere began to breed within their hearts.

After the initial confusion, they quickly understood. This was the fortune Li Fan had brought them.

So, they all knelt respectfully, expressing their reverence and submission.

"Rise."

"I do not need your kneeling. What I need is..."

"To tear apart this sky with me!"

Li Fan pointed at the net-like sky and said indifferently.

There seemed to be an unresolvable emotion building in his heart. After a moment of dead silence, the created beings roared towards the sky in unison.

At that instant, their voices shook the cosmos and resounded through the heavens.

Simultaneously, in the sky, a shadow seemed to flash past.

Then, boundless dark clouds began to gather.

Strong winds rose, thunder rumbled.

This was the net's response.

The legion of enlightened beings had indeed made significant progress in strength, but as long as they did not exceed the net's carrying capacity, the net could always create enemies that matched their strength.

The next wave of evolved malevolent creatures was indeed much stronger than the previous one.

This made the recently reinforced enlightened beings almost feel as if they hadn't grown stronger at all.

Without Li Fan's guidance, they spontaneously began to exert all their efforts to absorb and strengthen themselves amidst the fight.

And in Li Fan's eyes, within this vast net of this world, another smaller net seemed to be weaving and converging.

"Still not enough."

Li Fan said after observing for a long time, suddenly.

So, he increased the stakes again.

"Your nature is chaotic and ignorant. Thus, you can only kill enemies based on instinct, which will ultimately be your downfall."

"I have a method that I can impart to all beings."

"Absorb the essence of heaven and earth to gather your energy. Utilize the wonders of heaven and earth to build your Dao foundation..."

As Li Fan spoke each word, the countless enlightened beings present, their gazes towards the surrounding net-world subtly changed.

It was as if they saw the remains of the previous battlefield,

everywhere emanating fragrance, appearing delicious and alluring.

This world was created by [Net] in imitation of its past devouring of the Primordial Immortal Realm. Truly, every place held exotic treasures, and every patch of ground was a divine treasure.

However, they all transformed into sustenance for the enlightened beings' cultivation.

"Devouring Mountains and Seas..."

"Who can't do that?"

"If things don't change again, even with the foundation of the Primordial Immortal Realm, it will only end up like the previous Xuanhuang Realm."

Li Fan looked on with cold eyes.

Although he borrowed the name of the [Devouring Mountains and Seas] method from the past, it was not the same cultivation method.

The reason why the enlightened beings could truly devour this world was none other than Li Fan's deconstruction of the [Net] before him.

The essence of the net was another manifestation of [Net]'s past devouring of Mountains and Seas.

Li Fan held the [True Revelation Manual], having witnessed countless changes in the substance and illusion of Mountains and Seas.

Especially with the augmentation of Shou Qiu and Dao De's 'Concentric Unity,' his comprehension of the transformation of truth and falsehood, while not yet at its peak, could be considered a preliminary success.

After all, Shou Qiu had once, with a single thought, erased the virtual embodiments of countless Saints, and he himself could achieve Sainthood through the illusory forms of Mountains and Seas.

This world, originating from Mountains and Seas, made it not so difficult for Li Fan to deconstruct it.

What was difficult was finding [Net] hidden behind the scenes.

So Li Fan had been waiting for [Net] to rewrite the rules again.

Change begets flaws.

From only being able to devour evolved malevolent creatures, it had ascended to being able to devour the world itself.

The meaning behind this leap was significant.

[Net], who possessed the ability to Devour Mountains and Seas, naturally understood this principle.

As Li Fan said, if they continued to not change the rules and allowed these enlightened beings to run amok, this net would soon fall apart.

Representing the will of [Net], a drastic change occurred in the world.

Myriad bolts of lightning simultaneously appeared, illuminating the dim heavens in an instant.

The lightning did not vanish in a flash but seemed frozen, gradually filling the sky.

Like cracks, and like threads.

Dense and numerous, spread across the sky.

A moment later, infinite beings gushed forth from these cracks.

Their target was none other than Li Fan and his army of enlightened creations!

Even though they had no concept of fear, the net-evolved creatures, witnessing this scene, instinctively stiffened.

Just as their elder had said, the sky had been torn open by them.

But...

The number of enemies appearing simultaneously was simply too great!

Faced with such a terrifying disparity in numbers, no matter how they struggled, they would likely be easily crushed into fragments.

"At this point, what use is there?"

"It's merely a desperate struggle. Killing one is enough, killing two is a bonus."

While their minds were shaken, Li Fan's voice echoed in their hearts again.

This allowed them to barely regain their senses.

They retreated, forming a defensive line, gathering at one point, to face the surging enemy together.

However, the power disparity between the two sides was too immense.

The legion of enlightened creatures collapsed upon the first contact!

This was no longer the normal process of the net forcing a desperate struggle and then absorbing; it had turned into a complete purge.

Li Fan ignored the enlightened beings who were rapidly disappearing around him,

and even ignored the net beasts that were about to attack him.

"Finally found you!"

A cold glint appeared in his eyes. His movements were identical to what Saint Tai Wei had done previously.

He stretched out his hands and tore forcefully.

But it was not merely imitating the Saint Emperor's actions.

The Saint Emperor's desperate blow only exposed [Net] hidden behind the scenes.

Li Fan's true objective, however, was to carve off a piece of flesh from Him!

Not for Mountains and Seas, but solely for himself.

"First Saint of Mountains and Seas, I wonder how your path differs from the present!"

"Devouring Mountains and Seas, this Dao suits me perfectly!"

Separated by the Void Realm, connected only by the脉络 of Mountains and Seas.

The Saints in the Xuanji Ring on the Other Shore could only see the images transmitted by Li Fan. Li Fan, therefore, did not fear exposing his secrets.

With a single thought, truth and falsehood transformed.
